# Hotfixes

Hotfixes landed before 2026-08-26 remain in `docs/hotfix/LEDGER.md`. This lean
ledger records later corrections. Each row is committed immediately after the
correction it names, and both commits are pushed together.

The two are the same number for D under 24 and part by `(D>>2) - 5` from there: the old form labels a goal that the new one does not when its way takes between `5 + D + 1` and `max(5, D>>2) + D` steps, and a search that fails under both settles on different cells. The footprint side is `TokenSize`, the only size the search reads (`MOVE-SPEED-011`). `farBudgetFor` chooses the rule for all four of its callers: the movement pass, the flee test, and a creature's approach to a structure and its walk to one. A World hash moves wherever such a creature far-searches from 24 cells or more and the two waves end differently: in an A/B probe on EN (not committed, `review/hotfix-nxn-arm/probes/`), 5 of 921 drives of the hero onto up to 40 of the nearest hostile creatures in the 26 campaign missions differed within 1500 ticks, all on mission 120 and all through Ogre 30, in 13294 far searches of such creatures, 2684 of them from 24 cells or more, 15 of which differed; the probe compares the searches, not the fights after them. The bound allows 1 cell over the budget because the route read back from the labels can be longer than the generation count; in this drive the longest route of a large creature onto the hero's cell from 24 cells or more is at its budget with the change and 2 over before, so a bound of 2 would not fail at the base. The ordinary `go test` of `pkg/sim`, `pkg/game`, `internal/storyguard`, `internal/gatedtests`, `internal/divledger` and `internal/archtest` passes. `CommentBytes` is measured on this branch. Full final gates follow at the seat. |

Debt: a sectionless child record is written with Payment 0, empty AddHero and EnableMercenary, the values both producers already use (its MapObject 0 follows REG-GMAP-066's default); the registry reader's own default for each of these keys is not established in a claim read here.
